# Source parity ledger

<!-- GOAL12_LEDGER baseline=74caa2649f10ed71b4378ce69f5d9fbfd2466ca5 source=595/523/72/0 examples=99/99/0 -->

## 1. Scope and counting rules

This is the **source ledger**. It disposes every source file in the fixed
baseline but does not prove runtime behavior, non-functional properties, or
presentation equivalence; those independent axes live in `docs/parity-map.md`,
`docs/performance.md`, and `docs/presentation-parity.md`. The runnable-example
`examples/manifest.sexp` is an auxiliary inventory, not another ledger.

Source baseline: `earendil-works/pi@74caa2649f10ed71b4378ce69f5d9fbfd2466ca5`
(`v0.83.0-38-g74caa2649`), read from `/tmp/cadr-parity-74caa2649`.
The denominator is every tracked `*.ts` file below `packages/` except paths
ending in `*.test.ts`. This includes TypeScript configs, executable examples,
and non-`.test.ts` helpers under `test/`; it excludes generated/untracked files.
The sorted 595-path manifest has SHA-256
`242d90e03ae708b9ea30d71747052af80f6d19e62eec3990a5863c62a614965d`.
Its input is UTF-8 path text with exactly one LF after each path and bytewise
`LC_ALL=C` ordering; it contains no repository prefix, table prose, verdict,
or evidence columns.
The initial Cadr-side audit was made at
`fa6b09eda09a191ede16b7b15729da10a76e7f42`; Part C verdicts below are updated
only after their implementation and regression commits land.

```sh
git -C /tmp/cadr-parity-74caa2649 ls-tree -r --name-only 74caa2649 -- packages \
  | awk '$0 !~ /\.test\.ts$/ && $0 ~ /\.ts$/' | LC_ALL=C sort
```

Verdicts use no fourth escape hatch:

- `已实现`: the capability exists, with concrete Lisp symbol/file evidence;
- `有意差异`: a different Lisp/runtime/build structure supplies an equivalent capability;
- `缺失`: a material capability is absent, including reusable structure consumed as a capability.

A partially mapped file is `缺失` when any material capability in its listed surface is
absent. Barrel files are charged only for the public namespace; missing child capabilities
are charged to their defining files. Source correspondence does not prove internal behavior
equivalence, so this ledger complements rather than replaces behavior tests,
non-functional gates, presentation evidence, and user A/B verification.
Shipped runnable examples count as source capabilities; a lower-level extension API does not
make the example itself implemented. The deterministic image fixture generator follows the
same strict rule: embedded fixtures do not replace the baseline regeneration command.

All 595 baseline source files now have a disposition with zero missing or deferred
rows. All 99 fixed-baseline examples have functionally equivalent Lisp sources and
behavior tests. Parts C1–C5 closed all 19 reusable TUI composition rows, both Apple
Terminal modifier rows, all 22 agent/session/coding-core rows, all 36 provider
OAuth/transport/catalog rows, and both eval lifecycle rows. The Apple Terminal
path is 实现完成，平台不可实测: fixture parity is green, while this host has
neither macOS nor Apple Terminal and therefore provides no live claim.

## 3. Part C execution queue

| Suggested batch | Missing rows | User-visible | Refactor/dependency shape | Risk |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| TUI composition foundation plus dependent interactive components | 0 (19 completed) | Direct layout, loader, Markdown, settings and hint surfaces | Native CLOS component protocol plus dependent interactive components | Complete |
| Apple Terminal live modifier path | 0 (2 completed) | Shift/Enter identity on Apple Terminal | Direct CoreGraphics FFI plus ProcessTerminal integration; no new ASDF dependency, third-party library, or external binary | Complete (fixture parity; platform unavailable for live verification) |
| Agent/session/coding core utilities | 0 (22 completed) | Branch summaries, event flow, tool context and auth guidance can affect sessions/model context | Agent primitives before coding-agent wrappers | Complete |
| AI catalog, OAuth, transport and stream primitives | 0 (36 completed) | Provider availability, login and streaming | Native stream/catalog/transport primitives and provider adapters | Complete |
| Eval lifecycle integration | 0 (2 completed) | Developer/release evidence rather than normal chat | Native reporter lifecycle over the existing eval primitives | Complete |
| Shipped runnable examples | 0 (99 completed) | Documentation/developer onboarding | F/G/H close compaction lifecycle dispatch, structured system-prompt options, and the session-local extension HTTP runtime | Complete |

The 72 `有意差异` rows remain visible in the package tables below. The user
accepted them as a class and approved the queue order; extension-API gaps found
while porting examples still require a separate proposal and itemized approval.

## 5. Audit boundary

This axis detects missing source capabilities but cannot detect a function whose symbol
exists while its internal behavior diverges. The streaming-space corruption found earlier
is the canonical counterexample. The behavior, source, non-functional, and presentation
ledgers therefore remain independent, and real terminal/provider acceptance remains
mandatory.
